1b1('dul ba): @#//'dul ba rnam par 'byed pa//bam po drug bcu rtsa gsum pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal byed kyi 
1b2('dul ba): tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ban bzhugs so//de'i tshe na drug sde dag rang gi lag gis sa rko bar byed/rkor 'jug par byed/rnang sgal sgyur 
1b3('dul ba): bar byed sgyur du 'jug par byed/yur ba 'dren par byed/'dren du 'jud par byed nas/gzhan mu stegs can dag shes ldan dag shAkya'i bu'i dge sbyong 'di dag rang 
1b4('dul ba): gi lag gi/sa rko bar byed/rko 'jud par byed/rnang smal sgyur bar byed/sgyur du 'jud par byed/yur ba 'dren bar byed/'dren du 'jud bar byed de/bram ze dang 
1b5('dul ba): khyim bdag dag kyang rang gi lag gis sarko bar byed/rko 'dzud par byed/rnang sgal sgyur bar byed/sgyur du mdzud par byed/yur bar 'dren par byed/yur bar 'dres par byed/'dren du 
1b6('dul ba): 'dzud par byed na/shes ldan dag shAkya'i bu'idage sbyong 'di dag dang /brang ze dang /khyim bdag dag la bye brag ci ci yod/bsam pa ni ci yod/tha dad 
1b7('dul ba): du bya ba ni ci yod/su zhig dge sbyong mgo rig 'di dag la bsod snyoms sbyin pa dang bya bar sems shes smon par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar 
1b8('dul ba): byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byung ba dang 'di dang /gleng gzhi 'di dang /skabs 
2a1('dul ba): @#//'di la dge slong gi dge 'dun bsdu ba mdzad do zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mkhyen bzhin du rmed par mdzad pa ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa yang snga ma bzhin te/sangs rgyas 
2a2('dul ba): bcom ldan 'das kyis drug sde dag la rmas pa/dge slong dag khyed rang gi lag gis sa rko bar byed/rkor 'dzud par byed/rnang sgal sbyar par byed/sgyur du 'dzud par byed/yur ba 'dren par byed/'dren du 'dzud par byed par bden nam/
2a3('dul ba): btsun pa mang ngo //sangs rgyas bcom ldan dang 'das kyis dge sbyong gi tshul ma yin pa/rjes su 'thun pa ma yin pa/rung ba ma yin pa/tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin pa de ni rab tu byung bas bya ba ma yin no zhes rnam par smad pa mdzad do//de nas bcom 
2a4('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis rnam grangs du mas rnam par smad nas dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de lta bas na phan yon bcu yang dag par gzigs pas 'dul ba la nyan thos rnams kyi bslab pa'i gzhi bca' bar bya ste zhes 
2a5('dul ba): bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//nga'i nyan thos rnams kyis 'dul ba la bslab pa'i gzhi 'di ltar gdon par bya ste/yang dag slong gang rang gi lag gis sa rko 'am/rkor 'dzud na ltung byed do//yang dge slong gang zhes bya ba ni drug sde 'am/
2a6('dul ba): gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa'o//rang gi lag gis shes bya ba ni lag nas lag pas so//sa zhes bya ba ni sa gnyis te/rang bzhin can dang /rang bzhin can ma yin pa'o//rang bzhin can zhes bya ba ni char chu zhong na zla ba gsum gyis 
2a7('dul ba): 'thas par gyur pa'o//char chu ma zhong na zla ba drug gis sa 'thas par gyur pa'o//rang bzhin can ma yin pa ni/de las gzhan pa'o//rko 'am zhes bya ba ni bdag nyid kyis so//kor 'dzud na zhes bya ba ni gzhan no//ltung byed do zhes 
2a8('dul ba): bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro dang /yi dgas sam ngan song dang /ngan 'gro dang /log par ltung ba dag tu sreg par byed/'tshod par byed/ltung bar byed pa'o//gzhan yang ji srid du ltung ba de ma bshags shing so sor ma bshags na 
2b1('dul ba): dge ba'i chos rnams la sgrib par byed pa ste/des na ltung byed do zhes gsungs so//de la ltung bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong sa rko na rang bzhin can rko na ltung byed du 'gyur la/rang bzhin can ma yin pa rko na nyes byas su 'gyur ro//dge slong sa zhag 
2b2('dul ba): 'gogs na sra ba 'gogs pa na ltung byed du 'gyur la /grugs pa 'gogs na nyes byas su 'gyur ro //dge slong sa la phur pa 'debs na ltung byed du 'gyur las/sa las phur pa 'byin na nyes byas su 'gyur ro
2b3('dul ba): //shong dgod pa'i bsam pa la ni ltung ba med do//dge slong sa dang 'byar ba'i lci ba 'gogs na nyes byas su 'gyur la/lci ba 'ba' shig 'gogs pa na la ni ltung ba med do //dge slong dogs rtib par byed na sra brtib par byed na ltung byed du 'gyur 
2b4('dul ba): la/grugs pa rtib par byed na nyes byas su 'gyur rodeg slong 'dam skyod par byed na nyes byas su'agyur ro//dge slong 'dam dang 'brel ba'i phru ba dang /bum pa dang /ril ba 'degs par byed na nyes byas sun 'gyur ro//dge slong rtsig pa 'jig par byed na sra ba 
2b5('dul ba): 'jig par byed na ltung byed ngu 'gyur la/grugs pa 'jig par byed na nyes byas sus 'gyur ro//dge slong rtsi ga pa'i skyad nul 'gogs par byed na sra ba 'gogs par byed na ltung byed 'gyur la /grugs pa 'gogs par byed na nyes byas
2b6('dul ba): su 'gyur ro//dge slong tshig pa la phur pa 'debs na ltung byed du 'gyur la/rtsig pa las phur ba 'byin na nyes byas su 'gyur //dge slong rtsig pa dang 'byar ba'i lci ba 'gog par byed na nyes byas sa 'gyur ro//dge slong rig pa la ri mo 'dren par byed na nyes byas su
2b7('dul ba): shong dgod pa'i bsam pa la ni ltung ba med do//dge slong rtsig pa las tshwa rgo 'phrul par byed na nyes byas su 'gyur ro//dge slong rdo ba can gyis rko bar byed na//gal te sa ches mang la rdo ba ches chung na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//gal te rdo ba ches mang la sa ches
2b8('dul ba): nyung na nyes byas su 'gyur ro //rdo ba 'ba' shig skyod bar byed pa la ni ltung ba med de//dge slong bye ma can gyis rko bar byed ni /gal te sa ches mang la bye ma ches nyung na ltung byed du 'gyur//gal te bye ma ches mang las ches nyung na nyes byas su 'gyur

3a1('dul ba): @#//bye ma 'ba' shig rko ba la ni ltung ba med do/dge slong lag gi blas rgyu skar gyi sbyor bas rung bar byed pa med na thur ma sor bzhi tsam sal 'jugs pa la ltung ba med do//las dang po pa dang /smyos pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag gis nyen bal ltung ba med 
3a2('dul ba): do/sa rko ba'i ltung byed de bdun bcu rtsa gsum pa rdzogs s+ho////sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das shAkya rnams kyi nang du ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs pa na ser skya'i gnas su byon nas ser skya'i gnas na n+ya gro d+ha'i kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//shAkya ming chen gyis 
3a3('dul ba): bcom ldan 'das shAkya rnam kyi nang du ljongs rgyu zhing/gshegs pa na ser skya'i gan su byon nas ser skya'i ga nas na n+ya gro d+ha'i kun ga'ar ba na bzhugs shes thos so//thos nas kyang de da 'di snyam du sems te/bdag gis bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi 
3a4('dul ba): dge 'dun dang bcas pa la na mang du gdugs tshod la khyim gzhan du spyan drangs te bshos gsol na/lan 'ga' yang yo byad thams cad kyis spyan ma drangs kyis bdag gis bcom ldan 'das/dge slong gi dge 'dun dang bcas pa zla ba bzhir yo byad thams cad kyis sbyan 
3a5('dul ba): drang do snyam mo/de nas shAkya ming chen bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te mtha' gcig tu 'dug go/mtha' gcig tu 'dug nas bcom ldan 'das shAkya ming chen la chos dang ldan pa'i bka' mchid 
3a6('dul ba): kyis yang dag par ston par mdzad/yang dag par 'dzin du 'dzud par mdzad/yang dag pa gzeng bstod par mdzad/yang dag bar rab tu dga' bar mdzad do//rnam grangs du mar chos dang ldan pa'i bka'amachid kyis yang dag par bstan/yang dag par 'dzin dub cug 
3a7('dul ba): yang dag par gzeng bstod/yang dag par rab tu 'ga' bar mdzad nas cang mi gsung ngo/de nas shAkya ming chenastan las langs te bcom ldan 'das ga la pa de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi dge 
3a8('dul ba): 'dun dang bcas pas dgung zla bzhir bdag gi na bza' dang /bsod snyoms dang /gnas mal dang /bsnyung ba'i rkyen dang /sman gyi yo byad dag bzhes par ci gnang /bcom ldan 'das kyis shAkya ming chen la cang mi gsung bas gnang ngo//de nas shAkya ming chen gyis bcom 
3b1('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis cad mi gsung bas gnang bar rig nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga nas song nas des bsnyung ba'i rung ba'i khang pa rtsig tu bcug cing /bsnyung ba'i sman yang 'jog tu bcug 
3b2('dul ba): bde ba'i sman dus su rung ba dang /thun tshod du rung ba dang /zhag bdun pa dang /'tsho ba'i bar du bcad pa dang /rtsa ba'i sman dang /sdong bu'i sman dang /lo ma'i sman dang /me tog gi sman dang /'bras bu'i sman dag kyang 'jog tu bcug/zhun mang bskol ba dang ma bskol ba dag kyang 'jog tu 
3b3('dul ba): bcug/'bru mar bskol ba dang ma bskol pa dag kyang 'jog tu bcug pa dag dang /ma he mo dag dang /ra bzhon dag kyang 'jog tu bcu ga/yo byad thams cad kyang 'jog tu bcug sman pa dang snyung g.yog dag kyang 'jog tu bcugaste/dge slong dag la yang yo byad thams cad kyis 
3b4('dul ba): bstabs so//drug sde dag gis smras pa/da gwa bo nye dga' bca' ba dang bza' ba 'di lta bu rnyed kyis thabs shig bya'o zhes de dag gis sman pa la dris pa/bzhin bzangs kho bo cag la nad ci yod pa je ltos shig/sman pas ba sams pa/'di dag ni dge sbyong hral po dag yin 
3b5('dul ba): pas za 'dod kyis 'di dag dang mthun par smra bar bya'o snyam nas des de dag la smras pa 'phags pa dag khyed cag ni shul gyis dub pa dag lags pas je skur snum bag gsol la de'i 'og tu bska ba gsol cig/de dag gis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' 'di ni sman 
3b6('dul ba): pa chen po nang dang /nad kyi bzhis dang /khams la mkhas pa rgyal po la mkho ba yin te/gal te bdag cag thams cad kyis dus gcig tu de ltar bya ba brtsams na myur du zin par 'gyur gyis rdo dang por gnyis kyis snum bag bsten cing bzhis nad g.yog bya'o//de'i 'og 
3b7('dul ba): tu yang gnyis kyis snum pag bsten cing bzhis nad g.yag bya'o//de'i 'og tu yang gnyis kyis snum bag brten cing bzhis nad g.yog bya 'o//de ltar bya'o zhes de dag las gnyis kyis snum bag bstan par brtsams shing bzhis nad g.yog byed pa na ci tsam na zla ba 
3b8('dul ba): gcig 'das so//de'i 'og tu gzhan gnyis kyis snum bag bste na par rtsams shing bzhis nad g.yogs byed pa na zla ba gnyis pa 'das so//de'i 'og tu yang gzhan gnyis kyis snum pag bstan par brtsams shing g.yog byed pa na zla ba gsum pa 'das so//zla ba bzhi pa 
4a1('dul ba): @#//la thams cad 'dus te bza' bar brtsams nas zla ba bzhi 'das pa dang shAkya ming chen gyis bcom ldan 'das gshegs ston la spyan drangs pa dang nye dag sa phyag ched la 'phrin spring ste/bzhin bzangs deng kho po la khu shi dang /khu ba dang /sha chod dag gyis shig ces 
4a2('dul ba): spring ngo/phyag tshang gis smras pa/'phags pa dgung zla bzhi 'das te shAkya ming chen gyis bcom? ldan 'das gshegs ston pa spyan drangs na/khyed kyi bye ba da dung yang ma rdzogs sam/nye dgas smras pa/bkren pa shAkya ming chen kha shin tu chen pos sangs rgyas la sogs pa dge slong gi 
4a3('dul ba): dge 'dun zla ba bzhir yo byad thams cad kyis spyan 'dren na gal te spyan drang ba 'di lta bus sangs rgyas la sogs pa bde slong gi dge 'dun spyan drang du rung na ni nye dgas kyang sangs rgyas la sogs pa dag slong gi dge 'dun ji srid 'tsho'i bar du spyan 'dren par spro'o//des smras pa/khyed cag 
4a4('dul ba): zla ba bzhir sha dang khur ba sha stag zes par gyur kyang 'on kyang khyed cag smod par byed na/gnas brtan gnas brtan gang dag gis bzla ba bzhir a ru ra 'bru gcig kyang ma gsol pa de dag ni shin du dka' ba byed pa yin no//dge slong don nyung du dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag ha cang slong par 
4a5('dul ba): byed da ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de lta bas na dge slong gis sa cang bslang bar mi bya'o//dge slong dag sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na 
4a6('dul ba): grong med pa'i dgon pa'i gnas shig na drang song zhig 'dug ste/de'i bsti gnas de me tog phun sum tshogs pa/'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa/nyen kor phun sum tshogs pa yin no//de'i bsti de gnas de dang ha cang mi ring pa na rdzing chen po zhig yod pa de na klu gzhon nu zhig 
4a7('dul ba): 'dug go/gang gi tshe drang srong de'i dkyil mo dkrung gis 'dug pa/de'i tshe na klu gzhon nu de rdzing chen po de nas byung nas lus kyis drang srong de'i lus la lan bdun dkris te steng du gdengs ka chen po byas te 'dug go/gang gi tshe drang? srong de la nges par gyur pa de'i tshe na klu 
4a8('dul ba): gzhon nu des drang srong de'i lus la lus kyis bkrol nas dpung rgyan dang /rna cha gdu ba 'khor dang /phreng ba rgyu sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang /byug pas brgyan par gyur te thal mo sbyar nas mdun du 'dug ste/drang srong de la yo byad thams cad kyis stobs par byed do//
4b1('dul ba): drang srong de klu gzhon nu de'i lus kyi reg pas skye bo bseng ba seng par gyur cing ring ba dang /nyam chung ba dang /rke pa dang /lus ma thob par gyur to//dus gzhan zhig na drang srong gzhan zhig ljongs rgyu zhing 'gro ba na drang srong de'i bsti gnas su phyin nas des drang 
4b2('dul ba): srong de la smras pa/kye drang srong khyod kyi bsti gnas 'di me tog phun sum tshogs pa/'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa/nyen kor phun sum tshogs pa yin na khyod ci'i phyir skyab bseng bseng por gyur cing ring pa dang /nyam chung ba dang/rke pa dang /lus mthob 
4b3('dul ba): par gyur/des smras pa/de de bzhin te/kho bo'i bsti gnas 'di me tog phan sum tshogs pa/'bras ba phun sum tshogs pa/nye 'khor phun sum tshogs pa yin mod kyi/'on kyang 'di na nyes dmigs kying yod do//'di na nyes dmigs ci yod/des smras pa/rdzing 
4b4('dul ba): chen po 'di na klu gzhen nu zhig 'dug ste/gang gi tshe kho na bo dkyil mo dkrung gis 'dug pa de'i tshe na klu gzhon nu de rdzing chen po de nas byung nas lus kyis kho bo'i lus la lan bdun dkris te steng du gdengs ka chen po byas te 'dug go/gang gis tshe kho bos langs par gyur 
4b5('dul ba): pa de'i tshe na ngas kho bo'i lus la lus kyis bkrol nas dbung rgyan dang /rna cha gdub 'khor dang /phreng ba rgyu sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang /byug pas brgyan par gyur te/thal mo sbyar nas mdun du 'dug ste kho bo'i yo byed thams cad kyis stobs par byed de/de'i lus kyi reg pas kho bo 
4b6('dul ba): skya bo bseng pa seng por gyur cing ring ba dang /nyam chung ba dang/rke pa dang /lus ma thob par gyur to//des smras pa/khyod klu gzhon nu de phyin cang 'ong bar mi 'gyur par 'dod dam/des smras pa/'dod do//des smras pa/gang gi tshe klu gzhon nu de khyod la yod byang thams cad kyis stobs pa rang 
4b7('dul ba): byed pa de'i tshe na de'i mgo la nor bu 'dug pa de slongs shig dang klu rnams ni ser sna can yin pas mi ster bas na phyin cad 'ong bar mi 'gyur ro//dus gzhan zhig na drang srong de dkyil mo krung gis 'dug pa de'i tshe na klu gzhon nu de de bzhin du rdzing chen po de nas byung nas lus kyis drang 
4b8('dul ba): srong de'i lus la lan bdun dkris te steng du gdengs ka chen po byas te 'dug go/gang gi tshe drang srong de las sa bar gyur ba de'i tshe na klu gzhon nu nges par srong de'i lus la lus kyis bkrol nas dbung rgyan dang /rna cha gdub 'khor dang /phreng ba rgyu sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang byug pas brgyan 
5a1('dul ba): @#//par gyur te thal mo sbyang nas mdun du 'dug ste drang srong de la yo byad thams cad kyis stobs par byed pa na drang srong nges de la smras pa/klu gzhon nu khyod kyi mgo la 'dug pa'i nor bu gang yin pa de kho bo la byin cig/des smras pa/pha ma dag la dri bar bya'o//drang srong 
5a2('dul ba): gis smras pa/de ltar gyis shig/klu gzhon nu des pha ma gnyis la dris pa/yab yum gnyis drang srong bdag gi nor bu slong na de la stsal bar bgyi'am/de gnyis kyis smras pa/nor bu 'di'i mthus bdag cag gis zas dang /skom dang /spos dang /phreng ba rgyu dang /rgyan dag rnyed pa

5a4('dul ba): dang de bzhin bgo ba dag/nor bu 'di yi mthu las 'grub 'gyur pas/de phyir khyod la nor bu 'di mi sbyin/ha cang slong pas zhes sdang gnas su gyur/ha cang slong bas bdag la sdigs byed pa//ral gri rdo dang 'bru mar bdar ba bzhin//gal te khyod gnas phyin cad bdag 'ong na/

5a6('dul ba): ha cang bslang bar mi bya'o//dge slong dag gzhan yang sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na grong med pa'i dgon pa'i gnas shig na drang srong zhig 'dug ste/de'i bsti gnas de me tog phun sum tshogs pa/'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa/nyen kor phun sum tshogs pa yin no//de na bya mang po

5a8('dul ba): kye drang srong khyod kyi bsti gnas 'di ni me tog phun sum tshogs pa 'bras bu phun sum tshogs la/nyen kor phun sum tshog pa bzang ba yin na khyod kyis 'dir sems rtse gcig pa nyid thob bam/des smras pa/de de bzhin te/kho bo'i bsti gnas 'di me tog phun sum tshogs 
5b1('dul ba): 'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa/nyen kor phun sum tshogs pa bzang ba yin no 'on kyang 'di na nyes dmigs kyang yod do//des smras pa/'di na nyes dmigs ci yod/des smras pa/'di na bya mang po dag gnas pa de dag 'dir sgra 'byin par byed de/bsam gtan dag gi 
5b2('dul ba): gi tsher ma ni sgra yin pas bdag gis bya mang po dag gi sgra des sems rtse gcig pa nyid ma thob po/des smras pa/khyod bya de dag phyin cad 'dir mi 'ong bar 'dod dam/des smras pa/'dod do/des smras pa/tshes nyi shu dgu'i mtshan mo rtswa'i me sgron por la shing ljon pa dag gi 
5b3('dul ba): drung du song ste 'di skad ces sgong dag bza'o//phru gu dag bza'o zhes smros shig dang /phyin chad 'ong bar mi 'gyur ro//des de ltar bya bar brtsams pa dang /bya de dag gis bsams pa/drang srong 'di smyos par gyur tam ci snyam nas/bya de dag gis phru gu dag mdab ma skyes par 
5b4('dul ba): gyur pa dang de dag dong ste phyin chad mi 'ong zhing ji tsam na mthar gyis thams cad mi snang ba'i lam na 'khod do//dge slong dag re shig dud 'gro'i skye gnas su skyes pa'i srog chags de dag kyang ha cang bslangs pa na mi snang ba'i lam na 'khor na mi rnams lta smos kyang ci 
5b5('dul ba): dgos/de lta bas na dge slong gis ha cang bslang bar mi bya'o/ha cang slong na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byung ba 'di dang /gleng gzhi 'di dang /skabs 'di la dge slong gi dge 'dun bsdu ba mdzad do zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//sangs 
5b6('dul ba): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mkhyen bzhin du rmed par mdzad pa ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa yang snga ma bzhin te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis nye dga' la rmangs pa/nye dga' khyod zla ba bzhir 'gron du bos pa bdag gir byas nas de las lhag par bdag gir 
5b7('dul ba): byed pa/bden nam/btsun pa mad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyis dge sbyong gi tshul ma yin pa/rjes su 'thun pa ma yin pa/rung ba ma yin pa/tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin pa de ni rab tu byung bas bya ba ma yin no zhes rnam par smad pa mdzad do//de nas bcom ldan 'da'

11b2('dul ba): ba de ma bshags shing so sor ma bshags na dge ba'i chos rnams la sgrib par byed pa ste/des na ltung byed do zhes gsungs so//dge slong kun shes par 'dod pas kyang bslab pa de la bslab par bya'o//dge slong mdo sde 'dzin pa dang /'dul ba 'dzin pa dang /ma mo 
11b3('dul ba): 'dzin pa rnams la yang dri bar bya ste/de la de ni cho ga yin no zhes bya ba ni de la de ni rjes su 'thun pa'i chos yin pa'o//de la ltung bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong gis ji tsam cung zad dge slong rnams kyis tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyis bslab pa 'di la bslab par bya'o//
11b4('dul ba): zhes bsgo ba na de dag la 'di skad ces/bdag dge slong mdo sde 'dzin pa ba/'dul ba 'dzin pa dang/ma mo 'dzin pa rnams la 'dri'i bar du byis pa/rmongs pa/mi gsal ba/mi mkhas pa khyed kyi tshig gis bslab pa 'di la mi slob po zhes zer na thams cad ltung byed du 
11b5('dul ba): 'gyur ro//byis pa khyed kyi tshig gis bslang pa 'di la mi slob bo zhes zer na ltung byed du 'gyur ro//rmongs pa khyed kyi tshig gis bsla ba pa 'di la mi slob bo zhes/zer na ltung byed du 'gyur//mi gsal ba khyed kyi tshig gis bslab ba 'di la mi slob bo zhes zer na ltung byed 
11b6('dul ba): du 'gyur ro//mi mkhas pa khyed kyi tshig gas bslab pa 'di la mi slo ba po zhes zer na ltung byed 'gyur ro//yang dag pa kho nar byis pa dang /rmongs pa dang /mi gsal ba dang /mi mkhas pa yin na ltung ba med do//las dang po ba dang /smyos pa pa dang /sems 'khrugs 
11b7('dul ba): pa dang /tshor ba dag gis nyen pa la ltung ba med do//bslab pa la nye bar 'jog pa spong pa'i ltung byed de bdun bcu rtsa snga pa rdzogs s+ho////sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so 
11b8('dul ba): gang gi tshe tshe dang ldan pa 'char kas ni nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs pas dgra bcom pa nyid mngon du byas/tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa ni kau sha m+bir song ste snying las chung ngur 'dug/'gro mi gyogs dang /nab so gnyis ni dus la bab/dga' bo dang /nye dga' gnyis ni dge 
12a1('dul ba): @#//'dun gyi nang na 'dug pa de'i tshe na bcu bdun sde'i tshogs dag gdug cing pha rol gnon pa dang /mdo sde 'dzin pa dang /'dul ba 'dzin pa dang /ma mo 'dzin par gyur nas de dag gis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag yun ring po nas 
12a2('dul ba): drug sde dag gis tho brtsams te/ji ltar dga' bo dang nye dga' gnyis kyis tho brtsams pa ltar yang gzhan gyis ma brtsams la/ji ltar nye dgas tho brtsams pa ltar yang dga' bos ma brtsams kyis de gnas nas dbyung ba'i las bya'o//de dag gis 
12a3('dul ba): smras pa/'di'i phu bo rtsod pa la mkhas pa yin gyis tshur shog/je gros bya'o zhes de dag dpod sa'i khang par zhugs te/sgo bcad nas gros bya bar brtsams pa dang /nye dga' skar khung na nyan cing des de dag bsdigs te/kye ma bcu bdun sde 
12a4('dul ba): dag ci'i gros byed kho bo'i tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa kha cig ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das/kha cig ni snying las chung ngu kho nar 'dug/kha cig ni dus la bab pas de 'di gnas nas dbyung ba'i lasabya'o snyam du sems sam ci/khyed kyis 
12a5('dul ba): de kho bo cag zil gyis mnan te 'khod snyam mam/kho bo cag gis kyang khyed la mtshams chen po bcad par bya'o//khyed kyis glang po'i theg pa nyi ma dang /rlung gis yongs su bskams gyur cing /glang po'i gnya' nas lhung gyur kyang /lug ni lnga 
12a6('dul ba): brgyas drang bar byed/zhes bya ba ma thos sam zhes pa sdigs pa dang /de dag gis bsams pa/'dis bdag cag gros byed pa 'phrigs kyis gzhan du 'dod do zhes de dag me khang du zhugs te gros bya bar brtsams pa dang /nye dga' yang der 'jab 
12a7('dul ba): cing 'ongs te nyan cing des der yang de bzhin du bsdigs so//de dag steng khang du 'khod de gros bya bar brtsams pa dag/nye dga' bar khang du 'dug ste nyan cing des der yang debazhin du bsdigs so//dedag bar khang du 'khod de gros byabar brtsams 
12a8('dul ba): pa dang /nye dga' 'og khang du 'dug ste nyan cig des der yang de bzhin ba bsdigs so//de dag gnas khang du 'khod de gros byang bar brtsams pa dang /nyi dga' khyams su 'dug ste nyan cing des dar yang de bzhin du bsdig so//de dag khyams su 'khod de gros 
12b1('dul ba): bya bar brtsams pa dang /nye dga' gnas khang du 'dug ste nyan cing des der yang de bzhin du bsdigs so//de dag sgo/da du 'khod de gros bya bar brtsams pa dang /nye dga' bar rim du 'dug ste nyan cing de sa der yang de bzhin du 'di bsdigs so//de dag bar 
12b2('dul ba): rim du 'khod de gros bya bar rtsams pa dang /nye dga' sgo khang du 'dug ste nyan zhing des der yang de bzhin bsdigs so//de dag 'chag sar 'khod de gros bya bar brtsoms pa dang /nye dga' span spun dag gi nang du 'dug ste nyan cing nges der yang de bzhin 
12b3('dul ba): du bsdigs so//de dag span spun dag gi nang ngu 'khod de gros bya bar brtsims pa dang /nye dga' 'chag sar 'dug ste nyan cing nges nger yang de bzhin du bsdings so//de dag dgon par 'khod de gros bya bar rtsoms pa dang /nye dga' tshad tshing dag gi nang du 'dug
12b4('dul ba): ste nyan cing nges der yang da bzhin du bsdigs so/de dag tshad tshing dag gi nang ngu 'khod da gros bya bar brtsams pa dag nye dga' dgon par 'dug ste nyan cing des der yang de bzhin du bsdigs so//de dag lam nas 'dod zhing gros bya bar brtsams pa 
12b5('dul ba): dang /nye dga' phyi bzhin song ste nyan cing des der yang/de bzhin du bsdigs nas de dag gis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag gis 'di gnas nas dbyung ba'i las bya bar mi nus kyis 'di la bzod pa gsol bar bya'o zhes de dag gis de la smras pa/btsun 
12b6('dul ba): pa nye dga' bdag cag la bzod par mdzod cig/nye dgas smras pa/bzod par bya'o//de dag gis de la dris pa/khyod kyis kho bo cag gros byed pa ji ltar shes/nye dgas smras pa/gang gi tshe khyed cag dpong sa'i khang par zhugs te gros byed pa de'i tshe na kho bos 
12b7('dul ba): skar khung nas mnyan to/gang gis tshe khyed cag me khang du zhugs te gros byed pa de' tshe na kho bos bzhabs te mnyan to/gang gi tshe khyed cag steng khang du 'khod de gros byed pa de'i tshe na kha bos bar khang du 'dug ste mnyan to/gang gi tshe khyod cag bar khang du 'khod 
12b8('dul ba): de gros byed pa de'i tshe na/kho bos 'og khang du 'dug te mnyan to//gang gi tshe khyed cag gnas khang du 'khod de gros bye pa de'i tshe na kho bos khyams su 'dug ste mnyan to//gang gi tshe byed cag khyams su 'khod de gros byed pa de' tshe na kha bos gnas khang 
13a1('dul ba): @#//
13b1('dul ba): nyan rnas 'dug pa bden nam/btsun pa med do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge sbyong gi tshul ma yin pa/rjes su 'thun pa ma yin pa/rung ba ma yin pa/tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin pa de ni rab tu byung bas bya ba ma yin no zhes rnam par smad pa 
13b2('dul ba): mdzad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rnam grangs du mas rnam par smad nas dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de lta bas na phan yon bcu yang dag par gzigs pas 'dul ba la nyan thos rnams kyi bslab pa'i gzhi bca' bar bya ste zhes 
13b3('dul ba): bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//nga'i nyan thos rnams kyis 'dul ba la bslab pa'i gzhi 'di ltar gdon par bya ste/yang dge slong gang dge slong rnams 'thab par gyur/'tshang 'dru bar gyur/mi mthun par gyur/rtsod par gyur cing 'khod pa la dge slong 'di dag ji 
13b4('dul ba): skad zer ba de bdag gis mnyan te/de lta de ltar rjes su tha snyad gdags par bya'o snyam pa de nyid rkyen du byas te cang mi smra bar nyan rnas 'dug na ltung byed do//yang dge slong gang zhes bya ba ni shAkya'i bu nye dga' 'am/gzhan yang de lta bu gang yin pa'o//dge slong rnams shes 
13b5('dul ba): bya ba ni chos 'di pa rnams so//'thab par gyur/'tshang 'bru bar gyur/mi mthun par 'gyur/rtsod par gyur cing /'khod pa la zhes bya ba ni phyogs dang gzhan gyi phyogs su rnam par gnas pa la'o//dge slong 'di dag ji skad zer ba de bdag gis gnyan te de lta de ltar rjes 
13b6('dul ba): su tha snyad gdags par bya 'o snyam pa zhes bya ba ni de lta bu'i bsam pas gnyen pos de lta de ltar pha rol gnan par bya'o//snyam pa'o//de nyid rkyen du byas te zhes bya ba ni gzhan ma yin pa'o//cang mi smra bar nyan rnams 'dug na zhes bya ba ni cang mi smra bar rna gtod pas 
13b7('dul ba): 'dug de'i ltung byed do zhes bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro dang /yi dgas ngan song dang /ngan 'gro dang /log par ltung ba dag tu sreg par byed/'tshong par byed/ltung bar byed pa'o//gzhan yang ji srid du ltung ba de ma bshags shing so sor ma bshags na dge 
13b8('dul ba): ba'i chos rnams la sgrib par byed pa ste/des na ltung byed do zhes gsungs so//de la ltung bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong dag steng khang na gros byed cing dge slong yang 'dzeg na dge slong des rting sgra'am/se gel gyi sgra'am/mgul bsal ba'i sgra bya'o//rting sgra'am/se gol 
14a1('dul ba): @#//gyi sgra 'am/mgul bsal ba'i sgra mi byed na ji srid du sgra thos shing don mgo'i bar du dge slong la nyes byas su 'gyur la/sgra'i don gon ltung byed du 'gyur ro//dge slong dag par khang dang /'og khang dang /gnas khang dang /khyams dang /sgo khang dang bang rim dang /'chags 
14a2('dul ba): dang /dgon pa dang /lam nas 'gro zhing gros byed la/dge slong yang phyi bzhin 'gron dge slong des rting sgra 'am/se gol gyi sgra 'am/mgul bsal pa'i sgra bya'o//rting sgra 'am/se gol gyi sgang ma/mgul bsal ba'i sgra min byed na ji srid du sgra tho la don mgo'i bar du 
14a3('dul ba): dge slong la nyes byas su gyur la/sgra'i don gon lhung byed du 'gyur ro/nad dang bcas pa ma yin pa dang /gya tsom du 'jug pa dang /mnyan nas nye bar zhi bar bya'o snyam pa la ltung ba med do//las dang po pa dang /smyos pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag gis 
14a4('dul ba): nyen pa la ltung ba med do/nyan rna las gyur pa'i ltung byed de bdun bcu rtsa drug pa rdzogs s+ho////'dul ba rnam par 'byed pa/bam po drug bcu rtsa bzhi pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' 
14a5('dul ba): ra ba na bzhugs so//gang gi tshe tshe dang ldan pa 'char kas ni nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs pas dgra bcom pa nyid mngon du byas tshe dang ldan pa 'dun pa ni go sha ming song ste snying las chung ngur 'dug/'gro mgyogs dang /na ba so gnyis ni dus la bab/dga' bo dang /nye dga' 
14a6('dul ba): gnyis ni dge 'dun gyi nang na 'dug pa de'i tshe na bcu bdun sde'i tshogs dag gdug cing pha rol gnon pa dang /mdo sde 'dzin pa dang /'dul ba 'dzin pa dang /ma mo 'dzin par gyur nas de dag gis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag yun ring po nas drug sde dag 
14a7('dul ba): gis tho btsams te/ji ltar nye dga' bo dang nye dga' gnyis kyis tho ba rtsams pa ltar yang gzhan gyis ma brtsams la/ji ltar nye dgas tho brtsams pa ltar yang dga' bos ma btsams kyis de gnas nas dbyung ba'i las bya'o//de na kha cig gis smras pa/'di'i 
14a8('dul ba): phu bo dga' bo brtsod pa la mkhas pa yin pas bdag cag gis 'di gnas nas dbyung ba'i lasabya bar mi nus so//kha cig gis smras pa/gcig smra ba na gnyis kyis mnyan par mi bya bar tshogs smra na tshogs ni rgyal po dang 'dra bas gal te de cung zad smra bar 
14b1('dul ba): gyur na de'i tshe gnas nas dbyung ba'i las bya'o zhes de dag gis gnas mal bshams nas/gaN DI brdungs te dris pa'i tshig gis dge slong rnams la yang dag par bsgo nas dge 'dun thams cad 'dug cing 'thun par gyur pa na/gleng ba po rgan pa'i mtha' logs 
14b2('dul ba): su 'dug ste smras pa/btsun pa nye dga' khyod kyis bcu bdun sde'i tshogs dag gi lto dral te rgyu ma dag gis rgyal byed kyi tshal bskor bar bya'o zhes bya ba de lta bu'i tshig smras pa gang yin pa de dran nam/nye dgas smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa dag de ni don 'das pa yin nam sos 
14b3('dul ba): pa glengs te ci bya/de dag gis smras pa/bcom ldan 'das ni 'das pa'i don kho na la brten te 'dul ba la nyan thos rnams kyi bslab pa'i gzhi 'cha' bar mdzad do//dga' bos phyir bzlog par brtsams pa dang /de dag gis smras pa/gang gcig phyir zlog par byed pa de yang 
14b4('dul ba): gnas nas dbyung ba'i las bya'o//dga' bos bsams pa/'di dag gi byed stangs las na bdag la yang gnas nas 'byin pa'i las byed par 'gyur na ma legs par 'gyur ro snyam nas/de dgun gyi dus la bab pas la ba zhig gyon te des la ba de rtsig drung du 
14b5('dul ba): bslang ste bzhag nas cang mi smra bar stan las langs te song ngo //de dag gis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pas dag 'dod pa smros shig ces de dag gis gya tsom du nye dga' gnas nas dbyung ba'i las byes so//de gnas nas dbyung ba'i las/byas pa na ngu zhing phu bo'i gan ngu song 
14b6('dul ba): ba dang /dga' bos smras pa/nye dga' ci'i phyir ngu /lhung bzed nag po can dag gis bdag gnas nas dbyung ba'i lasbyas so//dga' bos smras pa/gang dag gis kho bo'i nu bo gnas nas dbyung ba'i lasabyas pa de dag grong dang /grong khyer dang /khams gsum nas gnas 
14b7('dul ba): nas phyung bar gyur cig/'on kyang gnas nas dbyung ba'i lasab byas pa dag la grong dang /grong khyer med dam ci sdong la phyis bzod pa gsol cig/'di ltar de dag gis mi mthun pa kho nar las byas so//su zhig der ma 'ongs/dga' bos smras pa/kho bo'o//bdag gis de 
14b8('dul ba): na rgan pa'i mtha' logs na slob dpon gyi sgra thos so//dga' bos smras ba/gang gi tshe de dag na re gang gcig phyir zlog par byed pa de yang gnas nas dbyung ba'i la bya'o zer ba de'i tshe na kho bos bsams pa/'di dag gi byed stangs las na bdag la yang gnas nas 'byin 
15a1('dul ba): @#//pa'i las byed par 'gyur na ma legs par 'gyur ro snyam nas/kho bo dgun gyi dus la bab pas la ba zhig gyon te kho bos la ba de rtsig drung du bslang ste bzhag nas cang mi smra bar stan las langs te song ngo /dge slong don nyung du dag ci'i phyir dge slong dag 
15a2('dul ba): dge 'dun gyi chos dang ldan pa'i gtam rnam par gtan la 'bebs pa byung ba na cang mi smra bar stan las longs te 'gro bar byed ces smod par byed/phyar ka gtong bar byed/kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol to//de nas bcom 
15a3('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis byung ba 'di dang /gleng gzhi 'di dang /skabs 'di la dge slong gi dge 'dun bsdu ba mdzad do zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mkhyen bzhin du rmed par mdzad pa ste zhes bya ba la sogs pa yang snga ma bzhin te/
15a4('dul ba): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyis shAkya'i bu dga' bo la rmas pa/dga' bo khyod dge 'dun gyi chos dang ldanapa'i gtam rnam par gtanal 'bebs pa byung ba nacang mi smra bar stan las langs te 'gro ba bden nam/btsun pa mad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas kyis dge 
15a5('dul ba): sbyong gi tshul ma yin pa/rjes su 'thun pa ma yin pa/rung ba ma yin pa/tshul dang 'thun pa ma yin pa de ni rab tu byung bas bya ba ma yin no zhes rnam par smad pa mdzad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rnam grangs du mas rnam par smad nas dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge 
15a6('dul ba): slong dag de lta bas na phan yon bcu yang dag par gzigs pas 'dul ba la nyan thos rnams kyi bslab pa'i gzhi bca' bar bya zhes bya ba la sogs pa snga ma bzhin no//nga'i nyan thos rnams kyis 'dul ba la bslab pa'i gzhi 'di ltar gdon par bya ste/yang dge slong 
15a7('dul ba): gang dge 'dun gyi chos dang ldang pa'i gtam rnam par gtan la 'bebs pa byung ba na cang mi smra bar stan las longs te 'gro na ltung byed do zhes bcom ldan 'das kyi 'dul ba la nyan thos rnam kyi bslab pa'i gzhi de ltar bca' ba mdzad do//dge slong na nga ngan can

15b2('dul ba): dang /chos gos bzhag nas ji tsam na der me shor nas dge slong des der 'gro bar brtsams te de lam gyi phyed du phyin pa dang /bdag gis dge slong 'dug pa lam smras so snyam pa'i dran pa skyes nas de phyir log ste ji srid du dge slong 'dug pa la sma bar byed pa de 
15b3('dul ba): srid du de'i ltung bzed dang chos gos tshig nas de der song ba dang khyim bdag gis smras pa/'phags pa khyod ci'i slad du 'phyis te byon/des na khyod kyi ltung bzed dang chos gos/tshig gis/gal te khyod myur du byon par gyur na khyod kyi ltung bzed/de dang chos gos 
15b4('dul ba): tshig par mi 'gyur ro zhes zer zhing dge slong de yang phongs par gyur pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de lta bas na de 'dra ba'i rkyen ma gtogs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 
15b5('dul ba): bslab pa 'dod pa dang /bslab pa la gus pa dang /bslab pa la yo byad bsnyungs pa'i bsngags pa brjod nas dge slong rnams la de dang 'thun pa dang /de'i shas che ba dang /de dang 'tsham pa'i chos dang ldan pa'i bka' mchid mdzad nas dge slong rnams la/bka' stsal

15b8('dul ba): gshegs pa'i nyan thos kyi dge 'dun gyi'o//chos dang ldan pa'i gtam rna ma par gtan la 'bebs pas byung ba na zhes bya ba ni gsol ba dang /gsol ba dang gnyis dang /gsol ba dang gzhi'i las byung ba na'o//cang mi smra bar stan las longs te 'gro zhing zhes bya 
16a1('dul ba): @#//ba ni thos pa'i nyen kor las 'das te 'gro zhing ngo/dge slong 'dug pa la mi smra na zhes bya ba ni de ni dge slong gang 'dug pa la'o/de 'dra ba'i rkyen ma gtogs te zhes bya ba ni de 'dra ba'i rkyen bzhag ste'o/ltung byed do zhes bya ba ni sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro dang 
16a2('dul ba): yid gsang na song dang /ngan 'gro dang /log par ltung ba dag tu sreg par byed/'tsho da par byed/ltung bar byed pa'o/gzhan yang ji srid du ltung ba de ma bshags shing so sor ma bshags na dge ba'i chos rnams la sgrib par byed pa ste/des na ltung byed do zhes gsungs so//
16a3('dul ba): de la ltung bar ji ltar 'gyur zhe na/dge slong ji tsam cung zad dge 'dun gyi chos dang ldan pa'i gtam rnam par gtan la 'bebs pa byung ba na cang mi smra bar stan las langs te 'gro zhing dge slong 'dug pa la mi sman ji srid thos pa'i nyen kor las ma 'das kyi bar du nyes 
16a4('dul ba): byas su 'gyur la/thos pa'i nyen kor las 'das na ltung byed du 'gyur ro/las dang po pa dang /smyos pa dang /sems 'khrugs pa dang /tshor ba dag gis nyen pa la ltung ba med do//mi smra bar 'gro ba'i ltung byed de bdun bcu rtsa bdun pa rdzogs s+ho//sangs rgyas bcom

252b1('dul ba): 'che ba dge sbyor ma yin no//mig ldan 'gro ba yod pa yi//nyam nga ba dag ji bzhin du//mkhas pa 'tsho ba'i 'jig rten 'dir//sdig pa dag ni yongs su spong //slar bar mi gdab gnod mi bya//so sor thar pa'ang bsdam 
252b2('dul ba): par bya//zas kyi tshod kyang rig par bya//bas mtha'i gnas su gnas par bya//lhag pa'i sems la yang dag sbyor//'di ni sangs rgyas bstan pa yin//ji ltar bung ba me tog las//kha dog dri la mi gnod 
252b3('dul ba): par khu ba bzhibs nas 'phur ba ltar//de bzhin thub pa grong du rgyu//bdag gi rigs dang mi rigs la//brtag par bya ste gzhan rnams kyi//mi mthun pa dang gzhan dag gi//byas dang ma byas rnams la min//
252b4('dul ba): lhag pa'i sems la bag byas te//thub pa'i thub gzhi rnams la bslab//nyer zhi rtag tu dran ldan pa'i//skyob pa mya ngan med pa yin//sbyin pas bsod nams rab tu 'phel//legs bsdams dgra sogs mi 
252b5('dul ba): 'byung ngo //dge dang ldan pas sdig pa spong //nyon mongs zad pas mya ngan 'da'//sdig pa thams cad mi bya ste//dge ba phun sum tshogs par bya//rang gi sems ni yongs su gdul//'di ni sangs rgyas brtan 
252b6('dul ba): pa yin//lus kyi sdom pa legs pa ste//dag gi sdom pa'ang legs pa yin//yid kyi sdom pa'ang legs pa ste//thams cad du ni sdom pa legs//kun tu bsdams pa'i dge slong na//sdug bsngal kun las 
252b7('dul ba): rab tu 'gro la//dag rnams bsrung zhing yid kyis rab bsdams te//lus kyis mi dge ba dag mi byed cing //las lam gsum po 'di dag rab sbyangs na//drang srong gsungs pa'i lam ni 'thob par 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnam 
252b8('dul ba): gzigs gtsug tor thams cad skyob//'khor ba 'jig dang gser thub 'od srung dang //shAkya thub pa gau ta ma lha yi lha//mi 'dul kha lo sgyur ba bla na med//'jig rten mgon po skyob pa mchog/sangs rgyas 
253a1('dul ba): dpa' bo bdun po dag/grags ldan rnams kyis so sor thar//'di ni rab tu rgyas par bstan//'di la sangs rgyas rnams dang gang //sangs rgyas nyan thos rnams kyang gus//
253a2('dul ba): 'di la gus dang bcas gyur pas//'dus ma byas pa thob par gyis//brtsam par byas zhing 'byung bar bya//sangs rgyas bstan la 'jug par bya//'dam bu'i khyim la glang chen bzhin//'chi bdag sde ni gzhom par bya//
253a3('dul ba): gang zhig rab tu bag yod par//chos 'dul 'di la spyod 'gyur ba//skye ba'i 'khor ba rab spangs nas//sdug bsngal tha mar byed par 'gyur//'dul ba rnam par 'byed pa/rdzogs s+ho////
253a4('dul ba): dbang phyug dam pa'i mnga' bdag dpal lha btsan po'i bka' lung gis/'phags pa gzhi thams cad yod par smra ba'i 'dul ba 'dzin pa kha che'i bye brag tu smra ba'i slob dpon 'dzin na mi tra dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba ban de klu'i rgyal mtshan gyis 
253a5('dul ba): bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab/'di la shu lo ka ni nyi khri lnga stong yod/bam po ni brgyad bcu rtsa gsum byas so////gnyis zhus dag/
